SEEDED Gwen P (ap_ratio 0.0005) + KogMaw W (ap_ratio 0.0004); the AD path is proven via a synthetic test entry (no champion mis-seeded with a fake AD term). ## Shipped/closed - relocated from Open items - Medium priority

- ✅ **DS review + augments - bonus-AD-zeroing augment: VERIFIED not an RC bug (2026-05-17 - do not re-investigate).** External report (different League project): an Arena augment ("Adapt") zeroes bonus AD → Maw shield / Endless Hunger haste / Death's Dance heal floor out; reporter hedged it might be a modeling-accuracy nit. **Confirmed against `effects.py`:** RC models all three as `defensive_only` / **non-DPS** - Maw `3156` (lifeline magic shield, no DPS), Death's Dance `6333` (bleed, no DPS proc), Endless Hunger `2517`+`222517` (Famine bonus-AD→haste + Feast omnivamp explicitly tagged *"utility; no DPS contribution"*). They contribute **zero** to DPS ranking regardless of bonus AD, so a bonus-AD-zeroing augment cannot make DS over- or under-rank them - there is no bonus-AD-scaled credit to distort. The one lifeline-family item RC *does* credit with DPS, Sterak's Gage `3053` "Claws that Catch", is correctly keyed off **base AD** (`bonus_ad_pct_base_ad=0.45`), not bonus AD - also immune. EHP path likewise gives no credit: shield throughput is an explicit Phase-1.5 deferral in `ehp.py` (lifeline family handled only as a dead-unique dedup, never bonus-AD-scaled EHP). **The other project's diagnosis does not lift to RC.** The real, *separate* gap is the already-tracked Phase-1.5 shield/heal throughput + P1b Arena-augment `as_pct` overlay (CLAUDE #26c) - RC currently gives no Arena pick-credit for these defensive items at all, a deliberate modeling boundary, not a bonus-AD over-rank bug.

A bound DXGI grab at the 1920x1080 <-> 1440p resolution swap is unreliable (the same reason the continuous DXGI screen-agent was retired in favor of the in-process self-grab relay (1-PC, ADR-011)). The `Matchmaking -> InProgress` transition edge is the symmetric game-start swap risk. **Surgical fix:** `_CAPTURED_GAMEFLOW_PHASES: drop "WaitingForStats"` (keep ChampSelect + InProgress); NEW `INPROGRESS_CAPTURE_DELAY_S = 15.0` module constant; `handle_event` sleeps past the game-start resolution swap before binding DXGI when classified topic is gameflow_phase/InProgress. Cherry augment_select stays (mid-game stable resolution). 65/65 tests PASS (+2/-1). **Carries forward:** future event-driven captures on Game-PC MUST exclude any phase that coincides with a resolution swap. - ✅ **s228 - DS Phase 5.9.28: conditional-target-state block_index schema lift - Part 1** (2026-05-16, ENGINE 0.99.0→**1.0.0**). The s225/s226/s227 hand-offs flagged pure-data registry work as **exhausted** (5 iterations, all 4 override registries swept) and the conditional-target-state schema lift as the only remaining DS vein - architectural, reserved for sign-off. Surfaced via `AskUserQuestion`; **operator chose option B** (the multi-session lift). This is **Part 1**, scoped exactly like s207's `int→int|list[int]` lift (schema + flagship seeds first; bulk seeding + live plumbing are follow-ups). **Schema:** `block_index` value widens `int | list[int]` → ALSO `dict[str, int|list[int]]` mapping a target-state condition → block; `"default"` (required) = the operator-commits/canonical-amped branch (s191 model); other keys = positive live-target-state descriptors selecting a **downgrade**. **Closed vocab** `_BLOCK_INDEX_CONDITIONS = {target_full_hp, target_no_cc}` (mirrors `_BLOCK_STRATEGIES`); unknown key **raises**. **Part-1 resolver:** `_select_blocks` resolves any conditional dict to `"default"` **unconditionally** - byte-identical to the equivalent int/list (**provably zero regression**); live predicate evaluation is **Part 2**. Server `_parse_block_index` accepts well-formed conditional bodies + skips malformed defensively (untrusted-body no-500; engine validator = hard enforcer for the trusted registry). **3 flagship seeds - all CONVERSIONS of shipped unconditional entries** (Part-1 provably no-op): **Zoe E** `{default:2,target_no_cc:0}` (live A/B 140.00 reg==forced{E:2}, 2× block-0 70.00), **Evelynn Q** `{default:5,target_no_cc:0}` (345.00==forced{Q:5}, 7.7× block-0; R:1 preserved), **Kindred E** `{default:1,target_full_hp:0}` (80.00 reg==forced{E:1}==forced{E:0} - honest full-HP no-op; the 7.5/5% missing-HP delta is Part-2's signal). Caller conditional dict via wire proven. Registry stays **125 champions** (in-place conversions). New `test_conditional_block_index_s228.py` (~52 tests) + `test_block_index_overrides` shape-pins migrated (incl. `test_every_value_*`→`_int_list_or_conditional`, mirrors s207) + 5 s223-s226 backward-compat pins updated to assert new shape + Part-1 equivalence (s223 KindredE class's 4 semantic tests pass UNCHANGED) + 9 ENGINE pin bumps. DS suite 2136→**2188**; wider RC **1107**; ruff+py_compile clean; DS restarted (taskkill 13452 → relaunch) → `/health` **1.0.0**. **NEXT (option B continues):** **Part 2** - thread real liveclient target HP%/CC into the ranking call (`coach_integration/archetype_dispatch.py` + state-builder + `/api/ds-preview`; `target_full_hp` has a clean liveclient signal, `target_no_cc` stays commits-default until a CC source exists). Plus a fast pure-data conditional seed-expansion batch (s207→s215/s217 pattern): Lux Illumination / Aatrox W chain-landed / Fiddle Q fear-state / more sleep/charm/mark amps. **DrMundo E is caster-missing-HP, not target** - needs a `caster_low_hp` vocab extension, flag for operator. s220 aggregator G Post-Game-Review reframe remains the big pending UI item.

- ✅ **s174 - Phase 1 Tank EHP scorer** (2026-05-12). New `agents/daemon_slayer/ehp.py` sibling of `dps.py`: `compute_ehp()` + `EhpResult` + `rank_items_by_ehp()` + `EhpRankResult`. New `/ehp` + `/rank-tank` server routes + `rank_tank_for()` / `ehp_for()` client helpers. `core/defensive_picks.recommend_defensive_items_via_ehp()` ships the Option B integration - curated `_DEFENSIVE_ITEMS` as `only_item_ids` whitelist on the ranker; falls back to heuristic when engine down. ENGINE_VERSION 0.62.0 → 0.63.0. Tests: 67 new (44 ehp + 19 rank_tank + 4 server) → 1022 DS suite (was 955); wider RC 839 green. Live-validated: Malphite vs 90/10 AD → Randuin's > Dead Man's > Sunfire; Nasus vs 30/70 AP → Kaenic Rookern > Warmog's > Jak'Sho. First of six archetype scorers per `NEXT_SESSION_PLAN_2026-05-12_ARCHETYPE_EXPANSION.md` (Phase 2 = Bruiser hybrid, Phase 3 = CS picker UI, Phase 4 = Mage ability DPS, Phase 5 = Assassin burst, Phase 6 = Enchanter HPS). Phase 1 deferrals to 1.5: shield throughput, healing throughput, caster-side enemy pen.

- ✅ **s175 - Phase 2 Bruiser hybrid scorer** (2026-05-12). Second of the six archetype scorers. New `agents/daemon_slayer/hybrid.py` composes `compute_dps()` + `compute_ehp()` into `hybrid_score = α·dps + β·ehp`: `compute_hybrid()` + `HybridResult` for the scorer, `rank_items_by_hybrid()` + `HybridRankedItem` + `HybridRankResult` for the ranker. Per-champion (α,β) overrides ship in new `agents/daemon_slayer/archetype_weights.json` - 20 bruiser entries (Jarvan IV 0.55/0.45, Darius 0.65/0.35, Riven 0.70/0.30, Mordekaiser 0.50/0.50, ... full list in CLAUDE.md item 32) + default fallback (0.50, 0.50). Ranker sort key is normalized percentage delta (`α · dps_delta/baseline_dps + β · ehp_delta/baseline_ehp`) so weights stay intuitive across the ~10× DPS/EHP magnitude gap. New `/hybrid` + `/rank-bruiser` server routes + optional `alpha`/`beta` override params + `rank_bruiser_for()` / `hybrid_for()` client helpers + `BruiserRankedItem` dataclass. ENGINE_VERSION 0.63.0 → 0.64.0. Live-verified on Legion :8893: Jarvan IV α=0.55 picks Trinity Force first; Nasus with override α=0.2/β=0.8 surfaces Heartsteel + Warmog's in top 5. Tests: 44 new (40 hybrid + 4 server HybridRouteTests) → 1066 DS suite (was 1022); wider RC 839 green. **Next: Phase 3 = CS scorer-picker UI** (single session, pure UI, exposes 6-archetype dropdown in champ-select + in-game switch).

- ✅ **s184 - First-purchase archetype-mismatch soft-nudge** (2026-05-13). Closes the s176 Phase 3 deferral. New `core/archetype_mismatch.py` (~270 LOC) owns the evaluator: `compute_nudge_payload(coach, lc, lcu_snapshot, cs_archetype_pick)` is called once per /api/state and returns the `state.archetype_nudge` payload. Mismatch detection is dispatcher-driven (no hardcoded item→archetype affinity table) - calls `rank_for_primary_archetype(champion, primary, top=15)` once per (champion, game_session) pair and stamps "fired" when the operator's first non-trivial completed item isn't in the top-15 ids. Item filter denylist covers boots/Doran's/trinkets/consumables/SR-starters/jungle-pets (~28 IDs); operator's other 99% of items qualify as signal. Per-game-session dedup via liveclient's `gameData.gameId` (with synthetic `champion@start-floor` fallback); module-level threadsafe cache, restart-wipes. Default DDragon-tag picks skip evaluation (`source="default"` → no nudge) - only fires when operator explicitly picked or accepted a prior nudge. Three states: `pending` (no first item yet), `no_mismatch` (item in top-15, decision cached), `fired` (mismatch). Engine-down returns empty payload + leaves cache untouched so the next poll retries when DS comes back. `dashboard/_liveclient.py` extended with `owned_item_ids` (parallel int-id list to `owned_items` names) + `game_id` siblings. `dashboard/_state_builder.py` stamps `state.archetype_nudge` next to `state.cs_archetype_pick`. Two new routes in `dashboard/routes_archetype.py`: `GET /api/archetype-nudge` (diagnostic snapshot) + `POST /api/archetype-nudge/dismiss {champion}` (operator clicked the chip's X - idempotent; flips phase to "dismissed"). `dashboard/api_schema.py` gains `ArchetypeNudgeDismissRequest` + `ArchetypeNudgePayload` models. ENGINE_VERSION 0.69.0 unchanged - no DS engine math touched. Tests: 54 new (40 in `test_archetype_mismatch.py` covering item-filter denylist + session-token + engine-mode mapper + 12 nudge-evaluator cases (happy / dedup / engine-down / default-source-skipped / pending / re-eval on new session) + dismiss + NudgeResult shape + 5 dispatcher-boundary cases; 5 in `test_state_builder_archetype_nudge.py` covering build_state stamp + empty + pending passthrough + exception-resilience; 9 in `test_routes_archetype_nudge.py` covering GET snapshot + POST happy/400/empty paths + route-registration smoke) → 1009 wider RC (was 955); DS suite unchanged 1426. Live-verified end-to-end on Legion :8888 after `echo restart > restart_trigger.txt`: `GET /api/archetype-nudge` returns `{"ok": true, "state": {}}`; `POST /api/archetype-nudge/dismiss` handles happy + 400 + idempotent paths; `/api/state.archetype_nudge` present (empty since no game). Dashboard JS chip renderer **deferred to s184.1** - backend-first ship matches s174-s183 cadence; operator can verify via raw `/api/state` until the chip lands.

- ✅ **s192 - Phase 5.9.5 token-variant block_index for Akali R** (2026-05-14). Closes s191 carry-forward (a). Akali R has 3 Meraki blocks - block 0 base + bonus-AD scaling (R1), block 1 minimum-HP R2, block 2 maximum-HP R2 via missing-HP curve. Pre-s192 the burst walker only knew base keys so setting `{"Akali": {"R": 2}}` globally would over-count R1. Extended the walker to do TOKEN-CANONICAL lookup first (`block_overrides.get("R2")`), falling back to BASE-KEY lookup (`block_overrides.get("R")`). API surface stayed `dict[str, int]`; valid-key set extended from `{Q,W,E,R}` to also include `{Q2,W2,E2,R2}`. Forward-compatible - future per-token entries drop in without code changes. Registry gains `"Akali": {"R": 0, "R2": 2}`. ENGINE_VERSION 0.76.0 → 0.77.0. Tests: 7 new in new `AkaliTokenVariantTests` class + extended valid-key assertion → DS suite 1658 → 1665; wider RC 1013 green. Live A/B on :8893: Akali burst 787.9 (forced R+R2:0) vs **941.7 (registry)** vs 1095.6 (naive R:2 only - over-counts R1) - registry +19.5% delta sits exactly halfway, confirming token-variant model captures the realistic mid-point. /rank-assassin top: **Lich Bane #1 +396.54** (Spellblade × doubled R2 max-execute × 90% AP scaling). `compute_ability_dps` unaffected - R2 entry is invisible to the mage scorer's Q/W/E/R-only iteration. **Carried forward to Phase 5.9.6:** conditional block_index based on target state (Zoe sleep amp, Lux Illumination, DrMundo E missing-HP threshold, Renekton Q Fury - substantial schema lift; defer until 3+ candidates accumulate).

- ✅ **s191 - Phase 5.9 per-(champion, key) damage block_index overrides** (2026-05-14). Closes a long-standing systematic under-scoring: 248 multi-block (champion, key, form) tuples in `champion_abilities.json` carry block ≥1 with amped/empowered/max/total damage; pre-s191 `block_strategy="first"` locked every caller to block 0. New `agents/daemon_slayer/champion_block_index.json` seed registry (12 entries across 11 champions): Cassiopeia E=1 (Total Enhanced vs poisoned), Veigar R=1 (Maximum vs executed), Anivia E=1 (Enhanced vs chilled), Diana W=2 (all 3 Pale Cascade orbs), Brand W=1 + R=1, Evelynn R=1 (Empowered sub-30% HP), Aurora Q=2, Belveth E=2, Karma W=1, Vex R=2, Ahri Q=1. Loader + resolver in `ability_dps.py` mirror the Phase 4e form_index pattern (`_BLOCK_INDEX_PATH` + singleton-cached `_load_block_index_table` + `get_block_index_for` + `_resolve_block_index_overrides` + `reset_block_index_cache`). `_select_blocks` extended with new `"indexed"` strategy + `block_index: int = 0` param (clamps negative→0, out-of-range→last). `compute_ability_dps` / `rank_items_by_ability_dps` / `compute_burst_damage` / `rank_items_by_burst` all gain `block_index_overrides: Optional[dict[str, int]] = None` arg + per-spell loop switches to `"indexed"` strategy per-key when override present (others honor global `block_strategy`). - ✅ **RC-DaemonSlayer task context - RESOLVED 2026-05-17 (`ef30b6f`, code fix, kept SYSTEM+startup).** Root cause: `tools/start_daemon_slayer.py:_log_startup` wrote only to `logs/` and did `except OSError: pass`, so under the SYSTEM-context task a boot-time write failure vanished. Rejected the proposed ops change (SYSTEM→Administrator + startup→LogonTrigger) - DS server is **not** supervisor-watched, so a logon trigger would leave it down on an unattended reboot (availability regression to buy traceability). Instead made logging resilient: canonical `logs/` → ProgramData fallback (`_FALLBACK_LOG_FILE`, always SYSTEM-writable) → best-effort stderr echo (`sys.stderr` may be unusable under pythonw - swallowed); `_log_startup` now returns the `Path` written or `None` and never raises. TDD'd (`tests/phase7_polish/test_start_daemon_slayer_logging.py`, 8 tests + 2 helper tests; full phase7_polish 43 green; ruff clean). Live-smoked: launcher's port-bound guard line landed in the real log **and** stderr. No scheduled-task change; no reboot needed.
